		   NULL, "Rika-chan terk edilmiş bir kedi yavrusu, kedi yavrusu. Onu eve götüreceğim!!!\"", Line_Normal);
	ClearMessage();

//MISSING SCENE BELOW
	DisableWindow();
	PlaySE( 0, "s_furu", 56, 64 );
	DrawScene( "white", 500 );
	DrawScene( "background/G1", 100 );

	SetSpeedOfMessage( TRUE, 128 );
	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { OutputLineAll("", NULL, Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }
	OutputLine(NULL, "むが、",
		   NULL, "Ah!", Line_ContinueAfterTyping);

	PlaySE( 0, "s_Down", 56, 64 );
	StartShakingOfWindow( 248, 40, 0, 0, 2*2, FALSE );
	StartShakingOfAllObjects( 248, 40, 0, 0, 2*2, TRUE );
	SetValidityOfInput( FALSE );
	Wait( 300 );
	SetValidityOfInput( TRUE );

	OutputLine(NULL, "むぐ、",
		   NULL, " Öğğk!", Line_ContinueAfterTyping);

	PlaySE( 0, "s_Kyupiin", 56, 64 );
	StartShakingOfWindow( 248, 40, 0, 2, 2*2, FALSE );
	StartShakingOfAllObjects( 248, 40, 0, 2, 2*2, TRUE );
	SetValidityOfInput( FALSE );
	Wait( 300 );
	SetValidityOfInput( TRUE );
	OutputLine(NULL, "ぎゅいぎゅい、",
		   NULL, " Of!", Line_ContinueAfterTyping);

	PlaySE( 0, "s_boyoon", 56, 64 );
	StartShakingOfWindow( 248, 40, 0, 0, 3*2, FALSE );
	StartShakingOfAllObjects( 248, 40, 0, 0, 3*2, TRUE );
	SetValidityOfInput( FALSE );
	Wait( 300 );
	SetValidityOfInput( TRUE );

	OutputLine(NULL, "みーみー！",
		   NULL, " Mi~ mi~!", Line_ContinueAfterTyping);

	PlaySE( 0, "s_tataku", 56, 64 );
	StartShakingOfWindow( 248, 40, 0, 2, 3*2, FALSE );
	StartShakingOfAllObjects( 248, 40, 0, 2, 3*2, TRUE );
	SetValidityOfInput( FALSE );
	Wait( 300 );
	SetValidityOfInput( TRUE );

	OutputLine(NULL, "　きゅい、",
		   NULL, " Mmm...", Line_ContinueAfterTyping);
	PlaySE( 0, "s_furu", 56, 64 );
	SetValidityOfInput( FALSE );
	Wait( 300 );
	SetValidityOfInput( TRUE );

	OutputLine(NULL, "すぽん！！",
		   NULL, " Güm!!", Line_ContinueAfterTyping);

	PlaySE( 0, "s_Down", 56, 64 );
	StartShakingOfWindow( 248, 40, 0, 0, 2*2, FALSE );
	StartShakingOfAllObjects( 248, 40, 0, 0, 2*2, TRUE );
	OutputLineAll(NULL, "", GetGlobalFlag(GLinemodeSp));
	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { ClearMessage(); } else { OutputLineAll(NULL, "\n", Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }

	SetSpeedOfMessage( FALSE, 0 );
	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode) == 0) { OutputLineAll(NULL, "\n", Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }


	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { OutputLineAll("", NULL, Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }
	OutputLine(NULL, "　かっこいい余韻も台無しだー！",
		   NULL, "Rena, az önceki bütün havalı imajımızı yerle bir ediyorsun!", GetGlobalFlag(GLinemodeSp));
	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { ClearMessage(); } else { OutputLineAll(NULL, "\n\n", Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }


	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { OutputLineAll("", NULL, Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }
	OutputLine(NULL, "　レナはいつものレナに戻ると梨花ちゃんを瞬時に、カメレオンばりの速度で捕食する。",
		   NULL, "Rena her zamanki haline geri dönmüştü ve avını yakalayan bir bukalemun gibi Rika-chan'ın üzerine atladı.", GetGlobalFlag(GLinemodeSp));
	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { ClearMessage(); } else { OutputLineAll(NULL, "\n", Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }

	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { OutputLineAll("", NULL, Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }
	OutputLine(NULL, "　梨花ちゃんはそれから逃れようともがくのだった。",
		   NULL, "Rika-chan kurtulmak için çırpınıyordu.", Line_Normal);
	ClearMessage();

	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { OutputLine("<color=#68aee5>知恵</color>", NULL, "<color=#68aee5>Chie</color>", NULL, Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }
	ModPlayVoiceLS(4, 22, "ps3/s09/22/171400001_1", 256, TRUE);
	OutputLine(NULL, "「はーーい、皆さん、早く教室に戻りなさーーい！！」",
		   NULL, "\"Hadi bakalım çocuklar! Şimdi herkes sınıfına dönsün!!\"", GetGlobalFlag(GLinemodeSp));
	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { ClearMessage(); } else { OutputLineAll(NULL, "\n", Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }

	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { OutputLineAll("", NULL, Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }
	OutputLine(NULL, "　知恵先生が職員室の窓から手を叩きながら大声をあげている。",
		   NULL, "Chie-sensei öğretmenler odasının penceresinden ellerini çırparak bize seslendi.", Line_Normal);
	ClearMessage();

	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { OutputLine("<color=#956f6e>圭一</color>", NULL, "<color=#956f6e>Keiichi</color>", NULL, Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }
	ModPlayVoiceLS(4, 1, "ps3/s09/01/hr_kei22890", 256, TRUE);
	OutputLine(NULL, "「行こうぜ、みんな！」",
		   NULL, "\"Hadi gidelim!\"", GetGlobalFlag(GLinemodeSp));
	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { ClearMessage(); } else { OutputLineAll(NULL, "\n", Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }

	if (GetGlobalFlag(GADVMode)) { OutputLineAll("", NULL, Line_ContinueAfterTyping); }
	OutputLine(NULL, "　全員が笑顔で応え、昇降口に向かって競争のように駆け出していくのだった。",
		   NULL, "Herkes birbirine gülümsedi ve koşarak sınıfa döndü.", Line_Normal);
	ClearMessage();

	CallScript( "&eyeCamp" );
	FadeOutBGM( 0, 1000, TRUE );
	SetValidityOfSkipping( FALSE );
	DrawSceneWithMask( "tips/gettip_tsum001", "mask1900", 7, 0, 400 );
	Wait( 5000 );
	DrawSceneWithMask( "black", "mask1900", 7, 0, 400 );
	SetValidityOfSkipping( TRUE );
